default function CustomersTable({
  customers,
}: {
  customers: FormattedCustomersTable[];
}) {

    <div className="w-full">
      <h1 className={`${lusitana.className} mb-8 text-xl md:text-2xl`}>

                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">

                        </p>

                        {customer.total_paid}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
```
